# E5 step 2: the swarm's reports

The Convert phase's report for each area it finished (`docs/loops/e5-loop.md`,
"Step 2 as a swarm"), copied from the Workflow's journal (outside the repo) so
the integrator and the lead can act on them: what each agent left as offsets
and what it needs from the lead (changes in files not its own, measure faults,
clashes). The latest report where an area ran more than once. Nothing here is
built or validated yet.

130 areas. Types, fields and unknown_ fields are the schema file's counts.

## accessories

- Check: schema_check: 778 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/accessories/accessories.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/accessories/accessories.h`, `itc_recomp/src/hooks/accessories.cpp`
- Left as offsets: An earlier run had already written itc_recomp/schema/accessories.toml and switched most of the hand port. In this pass I gave AccessoryMatrix three more fields (row0_x, row1_y, row2_z at +0, +20 and +40) and switched FromScaleRotationTranslation (sub_820EF660) to AccessoryMatrix and AccessoryFloat3 views. The lfs/stfs pairs are kept, so loads and stores are the same and in the same order. These offset accesses are left: (1) The entry slots, set + (((index+1)<<2) & ~3), in SetAcquireEntry, ReleaseSlot, and the hooks' Acquire, Release and SetFreeable: a register-indexed array of entry_0 to entry_2, kept as computed addresses. (2) The material's +176 and +144 stores in AccessorySetTexture: Model::Material.ret is shared and belongs to material-passes, so they wait for the second pass. (3) The texture set's +20 count: the return of a vtable slot-3 call, with no measure type. (4) Frame and stack words (frame+80/84 and the frame's back chain). (5) The Load/Store/Copy16 helpers themselves.
- For the lead: Not compiled (I had no build rights and found no compile_commands entry for the file). Please build-check accessories.cpp's FromScaleRotationTranslation edit. Measure fault: the measure joins Matrix::FromScaleRotationTranslation's r3 out (a 64-byte matrix) with the Accessory type, so Accessory carries unknown_4 and unknown_8. In C++ the function now uses AccessoryMatrix, and type_map.py would need to type sub_820EF660.r3 as a separate matrix to clear this. Also, the texture set that AccessoryTexturesVtable slot 3 returns (count at +20) has no measure type. Of the 16 unknown_ fields, 10 are the skeleton's job and the swing's words, 2 are AccessoryNodeLocal's +48/+52 and 4 are AccessorySkeletonTable's (sub_8259A400, not read). No doc rows changed.

## achievements

- Check: schema_check: 778 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/achievements/achievements.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/achievements/achievements.h`, `itc_recomp/src/hooks/achievements.cpp`
- Left as offsets: An earlier pass had already written itc_recomp/schema/achievements.toml (still untracked). It had five types: AchievementsPackedTime, AchievementFlag, AchievementPair, AchievementCourseTarget and AchievementEntry, plus 8 globals. Most of the hand ports were already switched: hooks' TimeLess reads through AchievementsPackedTime, and Check and Write go through AchievementFlag, AchievementPair, AchievementEntry and AchievementsGlobals. This pass added a sixth type, AchievementOverlapped: the write's 28-byte XOVERLAPPED, laid out as XAM's struct, which nothing in the game reads. I deleted kOverlappedBytes, and the write's overlapped address now uses AchievementOverlapped::kSize. Accesses left as offsets: (1) kProgressRoute (+6076) and kProgressTime (+2204) on the race's progress, [0x826D82E8], the measure's "Achievements" type. That type is shared, and race-states is first in its area list, so it is not this area's (seed didn't include it). (2) kItemWins (0x199FA), the SaveData::Of halfword, which belongs to another area. (3) The 4-byte arrays at the thresholds and user_counts globals (thresholds_address() + 4*i and user_counts_address() + 4*slot), u32 globals with no element type. (4) Check's frame words (+80, +88, +112) and every function's back-chain store, which are frames.
- For the lead: No measure faults. schema_check reports three image tables as "the measure never saw it" (AchievementPair, AchievementCourseTarget and AchievementOverlapped). Expected: these are .rdata and write blocks in the image, read only through globals. src/harnesses/achievements.cpp (not built since stage 5) still uses ach::kProgress, which no longer exists in achievements.h, along with kProgressRoute, kProgressTime and kItemWins. It is left alone. The race-states and save-data owners could take over kProgressRoute, kProgressTime and kItemWins in a second pass: progress +6076 is the route and +2204 the player's time, and save data + 0x199FA + 12*item is the item's wins (u16). No docs rows changed. Nothing was built or run.

## action-mappers

- Check: schema_check: 778 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/action_mappers/action_mappers.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/action_mappers/action_mappers.h`, `itc_recomp/src/hooks/action_mappers.cpp`
- Left as offsets: These are still raw offsets. (1) The listener's held word at listener + 272 is an input processor field, which belongs to input.md, so it waits for the second pass. (2) In MapperBindKnown, the input manager's listener list and logical pad (input::kManagerListeners and ManagerLogicalPad), plus the pad vtable slot read through VtableSlot, are input.md's. (3) ConfigureKnown reads the mapper's own vtable slots at +8, +16 and +28 (0x82007F78, an image vtable the measure never saw, so it has no schema type). (4) Array elements are reached through `<field>_address() + stride*i` as the schema's array rule says (raw u16s, kinds and links, repeat and curve pointers, the client list's counts, the mappers table at 0x826BAB80 + 8j). (5) The generic Counter() helper's word. (6) The std_list size word in the listener list. Frames and stack words were not touched. The 5 unknown_ fields are ActionMapper +2452 (12 bytes) and InputClientList +40, +44, +48, plus ActionPadValues +12. The header's removed k... offset constants (kWords, kConfig, kRaw, kRepeatPointers, kAnalog, kCurvePointers, kThresholds, kListener, kPadValues, kClientList) have no users outside the area. Every accessor the ports use exists in src/schema/action_mappers.h. I spot-read the diff: loads and stores happen in the same order and widths, and the pad pointer is still re-read where the original re-reads it.
- For the lead: Measure fault (needs a type_map.py change, which is yours): the measure files the originals that take the mapper's input state (r3 = mapper + 448: ResetStages, InitRepeats, ResetWords, Rebuild, Hysteresis, Words) and its analog stage (r3 = mapper + 1072: InitCurves, ResetAnalog, Analog, CurveUnsigned, CurveCentered) as ActionMapper itself. Their 31 accesses therefore land on the mapper's offsets 0, 16, 20, 24, 60, 144, 152, 156, 180, 300-312, 684 and 1404-1420. I followed boot-mode.toml's precedent and gave ActionMapper overlapping fault_* alias fields at those offsets, with notes giving the real member (for example mapper + 1852 for the first threshold), so the check passes. The hand ports use the embedded types ActionMapperState and ActionMapperAnalog instead. As a result, the lifted reemit will name these accesses fault_* on ActionMapper until the measure files r3 as the embedded types. ActionMapperState, ActionMapperAnalog, ActionThreshold and ActionSettings are reported as "never seen" by the measure: they are embedded or image types that only the hand ports reach. Most of the switch was already in the working tree from an earlier pass. This pass added the fault fields and verified the switch. Nothing was built, per the rules.

## ai-drivers

- Check: schema_check: 781 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/ai_drivers/ai_drivers.cpp`
- Left as offsets: About 515 simple `var + N` accesses and the indexed ones stay as offsets. Most are not this area's objects. The vehicle's +2432/+3444/+3900/+6148 and the like belong to the Vehicle owner. The race racers +20, the race data (+0xA3AF0/+0xA3AF4) and the race state (+2184/+6100) are other areas'. The route file +8/+12/+16 and positions belong to track_route. Frame and stack words, plus float or word pointers passed by address (speed, curvature, width, limit, index cells, `previous` cells), are not objects. These are kept on purpose: - Array-indexed forms: driver+172+40*i, driver+452+4*k, path+16+64*k, path+5164+44*k, the path's +5146 section list and ReactToOthers' ra/rb +172/+198. - Base-shifted pointers: RefreshPoints' and ChooseSideForOther's `entry` is path+64m with no +16, so its +56/+60 are an entry's +40/+44. LeftEntry's `at` is entry+16. The skill's trigger `state` cursor is skill+24+4i. - Extend's `record` is one variable used both as a RouteSlice and as an AiCorner. - KeepBranch's a/b (+0 halfwords) have an unclear owner. The 5 unknown_ fields are AiPathEntry +56/+60, AiCorner +24, AiOther +36 and RouteCornerEntry +0. The 26 fault_* fields are measure faults and are counted among the 260 named.
- For the lead: 1. The build needs the full `tools/schema_gen.py` run. `src/schema/ai_drivers.h` uses AiProfile, RouteCorner, RouteCornerEntry, RouteSlice and AiSkillSource before `src/schema/types.h` declares them, so ai_drivers.cpp won't compile until types.h is regenerated. With a scratchpad copy of types.h that adds those declarations, the file passes `clang++ -fsyntax-only -Wall` with no errors or warnings. 2. Behavior check: I compiled the old and new ai_drivers.cpp at -O2 with strict FP to scratch objects (no cmake or ninja). Both have the same number of byte swaps (1,595, about one per guest load or store) and the same image constants. The only differences are register allocation and address arithmetic. 3. How it was switched: a script rewrote 1,302 accesses in place, so loads and stores keep their order and width. Integer and pointer fields use getters and setters (`.x().address` for pointer loads). Float fields and CopyWord keep `Lfs/Stfs/CopyWord(base, View.x_address())`, and LoadS16 keeps `_address()`. Function-by-function overrides, read against each variable's declaration, set which variable is which type. ClearFollow's and ClearPlan's `plan` (driver+32) now goes through the AiDriver fields. 4. Globals: the 32 local image-constant `k*` were replaced by `schema::AiDriversGlobals` (`G::x_address()`) and deleted. kExtendReach was merged into other_gap (both 0x820027A4), and kDriverBytes became `AiDriver::kSize`. kRaceMode, kRaceWord (offsets in the race data, another area's object) and the normalizer function addresses stay. 5. `ai_drivers.h` keeps kDrivers and kDriverBytes because src/harnesses/race_world.cpp and racer_controllers.cpp still name them (harnesses aren't built). 6. Unchanged: src/hooks/ai_drivers.cpp (it has no object accesses; only a frame word) and docs/functions/ai-drivers.md (no rows changed). 7. Formatting: the changed lines were reformatted with clang-format (Google, 100 columns), and the include block was restored to its original order. 8. Measure faults are documented in the schema as fault_* fields on AiDriver, AiPath, AiOther, AiSkillSource and RouteCorner/RouteSlice. Examples: route-corner, corner-record and other-racer accesses filed under AiDriver, and the corner steps' records filed under AiPath.entries. These are for type_map.py. 9. schema/ai-drivers.toml was already present (untracked, from an earlier pass) and I left it as it was. I regenerated src/schema/ai_drivers.h with `--area`.

## alt-hud

- Check: schema_check: 777 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/alt_hud/alt_hud.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/alt_hud/alt_hud.h`
- Left as offsets: All of AltHud's and AltHudHolder's accesses and the image words now go through schema::AltHud, AltHudHolder and AltHudGlobals. These stay as offsets because other areas own their types: the UI movie nodes (+80, +108, +28, +36, +12), the Vehicle (+5712, +6252, +2944 and others), the racer records (rh::kRaceData), the car's block 2, and MenuList in menu_list.cpp. Frames and stack words are also left as offsets. The slots are reached as slot0_address() + 4*i, the original's indexed walk, because three separate accessors wouldn't fit a loop. src/hooks/alt_hud.cpp has no object accesses. I deleted the unused constants kShown, kTach, kBoost, kBar, kCar, kSlots, kPlayer, kInputRecords, kTen, kKmhFactor, kBoostCurve and kCurveTable.
- For the lead: The alt-hud schema and the switch were already in the working tree, probably left by an earlier run of this area: itc_recomp/schema/alt-hud.toml is untracked, and alt_hud.cpp, alt_hud.h and docs/functions/alt-hud.md are modified. I checked them again here: the diff only replaces offsets with views, in the same load and store order with the same widths, and set_bar(float(value)) matches Stfs. Field count: AltHud 14, AltHudHolder 2, plus 11 globals. tach_curve and boost_curve are bytes[112], and their +100 outputs are separate overlapping fields (tach_output, boost_output); the HudCurve type could later be a schema type of race-hud-object's. src/harnesses/alt_hud.cpp still uses the deleted ah::kCar, kPlayer, kSlots, kTach, kBoost and kInputRecords. Since stage 5 it isn't built, so it's harmless, but it would fail if it were ever built again. The doc paragraph in alt-hud.md is dated 2026-11-17. No rows changed. I didn't build, run the game or commit.

In app.cpp: the App's own +0..+128 words in App::App and FrameDriverSlot's interface4 + 20 (both app-states' App), plus frame words. In scene_target.cpp: SceneTarget's +0..+28 (scene-target's) and frame words. In hooks/app_start_up.cpp: VideoOutput's slot-7 vtable load on [0x8266D088]'s object (camera-modes') and stack words. In start_up.cpp: VirtualTarget's vtable-slot compares and kMovieClass 0x826C1CDC (movies'). The other files in src/app_start_up/ hold rows from other areas and I didn't touch them: audio_manager.cpp is sound-cues, movie.cpp is movies (and video-output for ScopedLock), network_set_up.cpp is system-services, text_init.cpp is strings-and-font. app_camera.cpp is cameras.md's rows: its switch to schema/cameras.h was already in the tree, it isn't mine, and I left it as found. Three measure faults, already noted in the toml comments: (1) App::SceneFromInterface8's r3 is filed under App itself, with its +16 as App.+16, when the offsets are really from app + 8 (AppInterface8). (2) App::App.r4.+0 joins the run loop with loads of its vtable's slots +4/+8, which land in AppRunLoop's tick_handlers bytes. (3) AppRunLoop is the same object as framework.toml's RunLoop and should be merged into framework's type. schema_gen prints "TilingTile (app-start-up): the measure never saw it": it's a hand-added embedded type for Tiling's +16 tiles and is used by scene_target.cpp. app_camera.cpp (cameras.md's rows, cameras area) was already switched to schema/cameras.h, including names that look provisional (unknown_400_address, NewAny_MessageVector_Allocate). It will break if cameras.toml renames them. That's for the cameras agent or a second pass. src/harnesses/app_start_up.cpp still uses the removed asu::kSceneTargetApp, kSceneTarget and kDisplayTiling; harnesses are no longer built, so this only matters if they come back. No rows changed in docs/functions/app-start-up.md.

- Check: schema_check: 778 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/app_states/app.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/app_states/app.h`, `itc_recomp/src/app_states/containers.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/app_states/containers.h`, `itc_recomp/src/app_states/layers.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/app_states/layers.h`, `itc_recomp/src/hooks/app_states.cpp`

I re-checked that diff: loads and stores are in the same order and at the same widths. I switched the last two message-word reads to AppMessageWord (StatesOnMessage's message and ForEachMessage's cursor). The only unknown_ field is App +114, a u8 cleared at construction and never read. Left as offsets: (1) WalkVector's vector+4/+8 in app.cpp, because it serves both the message vector and the app's request vector, and the request vector (12-byte {type, key, delegate} elements) has no schema type. ForEachRequest's cursor and cursor+8 reads stay for the same reason. (2) App::Start's stores to the boot movie list at 0x826C5BAC (+0..+28). boot-mode.toml owns these globals and that area isn't done yet. (3) The 0x826364EC store (kStartFlag). No schema file describes this word; it's mostly the title screen's and the type 6 and 7 modes'. (4) The iterator result stores at hooks/app_states.cpp:396-397, and every frame and stack word.
- For the lead: The deleted offset constants (kStatesRoot, kInterface*, kAppMaps, kAppRequests/Messages/Message/Interface, kVector*, kList*, kNode*, kOpNodeBytes, kDelegateBytes, kLayerOps/Owner/Children/States, kLayerBytes, kStatesBytes) are still used by src/harnesses/app_states.cpp. That file isn't built since stage 5, so the build doesn't break. Change it only if harnesses ever come back. Globals for other areas: boot-mode.toml already has movie_ids, next_type, skippable and movie_index at 0x826C5BAC..C8, so App::Start (src/app_states/layers.cpp) can switch to BootModeGlobals once boot-mode is done. 0x826364EC (which front-end menu to show on return, probably) is in no schema yet. It belongs to whichever area owns it (title-screen or type6-type7-modes). Measure faults are recorded as fault_* fields with notes in the toml: for_each and iterator result temporaries joined with App, MessageVector, OpList and PtrList, and the app's +4 and +20 interfaces joined with App. The lead still needs to build: nothing was compiled here.

## archive

- Check: schema_check: 778 types, 0 accesses fit no field
- Files switched: `itc_recomp/src/archive/archive.cpp`, `itc_recomp/src/archive/archive.h`, `itc_recomp/src/hooks/archive.cpp`, `docs/functions/archive.md`
- Left as offsets: archive.cpp: the 40-byte request copy to the slot's +12 (byte stores from slot.flags_address(), as the original's bytes), the zeroing of +52..+88 (a word loop from state_address() to unpack_destination_address()), the staging half picked by a computed index (from staging_halves_address()), the store through the caller's word (r.destination(), not an object), and Ucl::Unpack's stream and blocks (a compressed data format, not an object). hooks/archive.cpp: the loose read's frame words (+80, +84, +88: stack, step 6). src/archive/semaphores.cpp/.h: the semaphores' count at +32, which belongs to video-output.md's Semaphore class (another area's type). The Font name is misleading: its accesses are Surface::GetDesc's render-target path. - BufferHeader: VertexBuffer, IndexBuffer and both new<32>. - LevelDesc: Texture::LevelDesc.r5 and Surface::GetDesc.r4. - EdramAllocator: "[82953E20]" and "new<804>@Edram::Alloc". - GpuOutWord: 23 out-argument measures (r4 to r10 words, plus "TextureLayout", which is LevelSize's in-out word).  Measure faults: - TextureHeader is shared with car-models.md (1 function) but is described here. - Edram::Mark.r3 and Edram::NextRun.r3 (size 0, +[i]) are the bitmap's words. They aren't seeded or claimed.  Other changes: - edram.h: kAllocator, kFree, kBitmap and kBitmapEnd are deleted. The retired, unbuilt src/harnesses/graphics_resources.cpp still names them, so it would need the views if it were ever built again. - [globals]: device (0x820007B0, ptr to Device), hi_z_taken (0x829537C4), edram_allocator (0x82953E20) and format_table (0x82013468). - No rows in docs/functions/graphics-resources.md were changed.

In RaceHud::RunMovie (sub_822141E8), the tail call `b 0x82212958` (state 3) is immediately followed by the label for states 2 and 4 (`lwz r3,632(r3)`). type_map.py runs from that tail call into the next label, so it took r3 there as sub_82212958's return value. In fact r3 is still the HUD. I described it in itc_recomp/schema/race-hud.toml as [RaceHudRunMovieTail], with field state2_movie at +632 and a note explaining this. It should move into race-hud-object's RaceHud (`measures = [..., "sub_82212958.ret"]`) and be deleted from my file once that type exists, or type_map.py should stop running from an unconditional `b` into the next label. RaceHud field names from docs/functions/race-hud.md, for race-hud-object's agent: +444 a pointer to an object whose +3932 is probably the race's tick count; +448 state (u32: 0-4 handled, 5 and above do nothing); +456 run-or-draw flag for state 0; +604 the state 0 movie; +624 the timer movie (state 1); +632 the state 2/4 movie; +636 the second movie state 3 draws (sub_82212958). Once RaceHud and RivalLogo have views, src/race_hud/race_hud.cpp's 7 loads and stores, plus the one load of entry+8 in src/hooks/race_hud.cpp, can be switched. I left docs/functions/race-hud.md unchanged: I changed or added no rows.

- Left as offsets: I left these accesses as offsets: - **Loops over offset tables or ranges:**   - `FromBuild`'s `kSettings` and `kTuning` tables.   - `ResetSettings`' bytes +78 to +92 and its nine halfwords (addressed from `setting_half_0_address()`).   - `TuningReset`'s words +20 to +164.   - `SetModel`'s gear ratios, the five words at +1568, and the wheel and inch-up pairs (addressed from the view's `_address()` plus a stride). - **Stack frames and words (not objects):** `frame + 80/82`, the course route copies, and the racer records built at `frame + N`. - **Other areas' types, left for the second pass:**   - The race data: +20, +24, +28, +0xA3AD0 to +0xA3AF4 and +0x59AE4.   - The racer array (`Racers`): +0x595E0 to +0x595EC.   - Stages: +800, +804, the 80-byte stages and their slots.   - `CourseRecord::Reset`'s halfwords.   - RaceEvent: +20 and +156.   - The data tables' item, the car build, the spec (`Params::GetCarSpec.ret`), the save data and GarageCar (`car + 2104`).
- For the lead: The check passes only because of two `bytes` fields that soak up measure faults. Both are Car fields in `itc_recomp/schema/race-setup.toml`, each with a note saying why: - **`fault_head` (+0, 72 bytes):** callees that get `car + N` are filed as Car at +0. These are `Car::ResetSettings` (car+16), `Car::ResetDecals6` and `ResetDecals40` (+96, +288) and `sub_820CCC70` (+1568). The fix belongs in `tools/type_map.py`: these callees' r3 should be the sub-object at an offset, not Car at 0. - **`fault_scene_model` (+4288, 832 bytes):** the measure joins three objects into the "Car" measure. `Car::DrawUpdate`'s r3 is really the scene model, and `Car::TickUpdate`'s are the race scene's car slots (offsets +0, +16, +32, +4288 and +4992 to +5116). The real car object is 3,936 bytes, and the size is set to that. These functions' "Car" should be SceneModel or a car slot.  The measure also files GarageCar's +1884 and +1900 under Car (from `Car::SetModelAndClass`). I named them `garage_class` and `garage_price`.  Other points for you: - **Duplicate types:** CarDecal (32 bytes) and CarChoice (12 bytes) in my file have the same layouts as `car-makers.toml`'s `CarMakersCarDecal` and `CarMakersCarChoice`. `CarMakersCar`, `PhysicsCar` and `GarageCar` are other views of the same car object. Build note: src/schema/types.h currently lacks forward declarations for GameMode, ModeDelegate, MenuList and my types. The full schema_gen run fixes that. A clang -fsyntax-only check of all my files passed against a private types.h with those declarations added. Some other agent overwrote a shim I had first put directly in the shared scratchpad, so agents there should use their own subfolders. One order change, matching the original: where a value load and a load of the title's pointer used to sit together in one Store(...) call (argument order unspecified, clang loads the pointer first), the value is now computed first. This applies to SetUpMainMenu's +12 (the save's +6092 first) and MainMenu's +4 (ListSelection first, via SetMainItem). The generated originals load in that order. Only two reads swap places; no store moves. In ForceExit, the block pointer is now loaded before the stack doubleword, as clang evaluated it before. New helpers: ResetMenu (FrontEndMenu, used by Create and SetUpMenu4) and ListAddress, both in internal.h, plus InstallDelegate and ShownState in mode.cpp. Their stores keep the original order. No rows changed in docs/functions/title-screen.md.

It is filed under TrackRouteHalfword with a note. - In-flight measures `TrackRoute::LinkDistance.ret`/`.ret.+0` and `Normalize.ret`/`.ret.+0` are TrackRoute and its file, merged into those types. - Other areas' files, for your second pass: - camera-modes, race-states, minimap, race-world and race-event each declare a global `route` at 0x826C5B10 as u32 (race-event as ptr). race-loading has `route_state` at 0x826C5B18. - My [globals] has `route_file` at 0x826C5B10, a ptr to TrackRouteFile, so those could point at TrackRouteFile. - Their accesses to the route's sections and slices could use TrackRouteSection and TrackRouteSlice. - src/track_collision/track_world.cpp still reads `kRoute + 8`, which is TrackRoute's `state`. - TrackRouteFile's size is set to 68 from known-data's layout (+20 holds twelve tables). So I wrote XACTVoice, XACTReverb and XACTWave from docs/known-data.md and the hand port, with no `measure` names. schema_check reports all three as "the measure never saw it". That is expected until type_map.py types the waves' r3, for example the XACTWave measure for sub_825D23F0, sub_825D37A8 and the vtable-0x82076A74 slots, and the XACTVoice measure for sub_825C9C40 and the vtable-0x82075B98 slots. Decision taken: XACTWave is a single 96-byte type, the base fields plus the in-memory wave's +64 to +92 fields, because every wave seen in play is an in-memory wave and the same pointer goes to both the base and the in-memory functions. The streaming wave (never in play, different fields from +64 to at least +176) would need its own type later. Pointers into my own types are `ptr` with `to` (XACTWave.voice to XACTVoice, next_wave to XACTWave), so the code reads them with `.address`. Pointers to other areas' objects (engine, owner, wave_bank, source_voice) are u32 with notes. Over to the lead: build voices.cpp and waves.cpp (not compiled in the build); the full schema_gen run, since types.h has no forward declarations for these types yet; the lifted code. No rows in docs/functions/xact-waves.md were changed.

Separately, xaudio-engine.toml and xaudio-objects.toml both seem to declare [XAudioAllocator], which is a clash between those two files. XAudioSourceCodec is xaudio-objects' XAudioCodec as met through the voice's +76. XAudioEffectObject and XAudioOutputMatrix are XAudioObject and XAudioMatrix. XAudioEngine.allocator points to xaudio-engine's XAudioBumpAllocator. 4) Measure faults, kept as noted alias fields: (a) a vtable is taken for the object itself, because the voice's +0 is said to point to a voice, so vtable slot loads land on the voice, codec, allocator, targets and matrices (the vt_* fields). (b) Descriptions are joined with the voices in BaseSize, OutputsSize, SourceVoice::Init/Size, SubmixVoice::Describe and Buffer::Size (the desc_* fields). (c) XAudioEngine::Init/Size's params and Barrier's 8-byte barrier are joined with XAudioEngine at +0..+4. (d) The measure 'XAudio' is the source voice description, joined with WaitForVoice's engine at +144/+300. (e) XAudioObject::Free's object is really a voice, but the measure filed it under xaudio-objects' XAudioObject, whose +8 is ref_count; the real +8 there is the voice's allocator. Both tools now run clean. Per the rules, I didn't build, compile, run the game, lift, or run any git command that changes anything.

That includes the predication, bin mask, tiling flags, write position, soft end, tiling end and generation, tails, flags/flags2, tap, options, the worker_* shared-state words, last_worker_cpu, worker_callback_argument and the chain addresses. The chains' cursor/end/first and the 20-byte chain copy go through schema::CommandChain: the five fields are loaded and stored in the same order as the old word loop. The hooks' device + 13444 is now Device::front_buffer_header_address(). The device-field constants that nothing uses any more were deleted from predicated_tiling.h. Loads and stores keep the same order and widths.
- For the lead: Nothing was built. Both .cpp files passed a clang++ -fsyntax-only -Wall check (not a build) with no errors or warnings. 1) Measure fault in graphics-resources.toml: the global `device = { address = 0x820007B0, type = "ptr", to = "Device" }` is wrong. 0x820007B0 is the import slot holding the address of the kernel's VdGlobalDevice variable, so the device is [[0x820007B0]], and G::device() gives the variable's address, not the Device. It needs a pointer-to-pointer type or a different name. predicated_tiling.cpp keeps its own kVdGlobalDeviceImport double load until then. hooks/graphics_device.cpp's comment and constant say the same thing. 2) The worker blocks (56 bytes a CPU at device +11212) have no type. device-state.toml only describes CPU 0's (worker0_*). A WorkerBlock type would let the block accesses switch: +0 the state, +4 the cpu, +8/+16 the slices, +20 the end, +24/+28 the signal words, +32 the KEVENT, +48 the thread handle, +52 the thread id. Measure fault already reported: Device::WorkerThread's r3 is the block, not the Device. 3) The command_buffer.h constants kChainCursor, kChainEnd and kChainFirst, and the device constants kWritePosition, kSoftEnd, kTilingEnd, kTilingGeneration, kTailPosition, kTailGeneration, kFlags, kTailsDirect and kSegmentChain, are no longer used by predicated-tiling. Whether to delete them is command-buffer's call. 4) No docs/functions/predicated-tiling.md rows were changed. The schema file is unchanged from the first pass: 6 types, 58 field entries, 6 unknown_, so 52 named. The earlier leftovers (adding measures to graphics-resources TextureHeader/GpuOutWord, device-state DeviceGammaRamp and command-buffer CommandWritePosition) still stand, as in the first report.

(b) Player 0's save data (race-event's SaveData): the options' hud_option_0-3, the bytes +28/+29 and setting_0-2, plus day and money. (c) The stats copy, through career's CareerSave::stats_address. (d) The mapper entries (racer-controllers' PlayerMapperEntry and RacerControllersGlobals). (e) The race data's stages and kind (race-states' RaceData via RaceStatesGlobals). (f) The save buffers' replay and replay_record (save-interface's SaveBuffers). (g) Replay::CopyFrom's blocks (type10-mode's KeptReplay). Same loads and stores, same widths and order. I also deleted the unused Get() helpers in dialog.cpp and options.cpp. clang++ -fsyntax-only with the build's flags is clean on all 9 of my .cpp files. Nothing was built.
- For the lead: (1) The volume bytes have clashing names. race-event.toml's SaveData calls options +28 effects_volume and +29 music_volume. race-screens.toml (PauseMenuData's music_volume_saved, from +28) and the PauseMenu::LoadData row say +28 is the music. So the code now reads set_effects_volume(music_volume()); there is a comment at each use. The lead should pick one name and fix the other toml. (2) Missing field: race-event's SaveData has no stats field at +48. I used career.toml's CareerSave for it. SaveData and CareerSave describe the same 129,400-byte object, so they could be merged. (3) Missing field: the vehicle (Vehicle::Get) needs fields at +6244 and +6248, two floats PauseMenu::LoadData clamps for the career screen. No toml names them. (4) UiMovieNode (tuning-screen.toml) has +100/+104 only as the f32 width and height. A group's first child index and child count read them as words. A u32 alias (e.g. first_child, child_count) would let race_screens/ui_node.h drop its _address() loads. (5) The kControllerEntries, kMapperEntries and kRaceData constants stay in race_screens.h, since race-screens-unseen's results.cpp and kind6_pages.cpp still use them.
